- **Organization: the #2964 lane's kept sections, and the one real hole.** The lane adds Roles at `/{org}/roles` and Workspaces as a section of `/{org}`; Invitations stays a section, not a tab; model funding and routes, the data plane and SSO/SCIM are cut whatever #2964's title says (the issue was retitled and those three moved under its Scope note in the same change). Settled: **`create_workspace` has no rev1 surface.** Between WL-39 and the #2964 lane an org that wants a second workspace makes it through the API, MCP or CLI, not in the app; `create_org` makes the first one. Rev1 ships that way on purpose rather than growing a form no §1.2 row covers. Superseded on 2026-09-15 by maintainer decision 13 (WL-62).
- **#2962 and #2970 become lanes.** Both were open `kind:gap` issues in no lane list while §1.2 attributed their contracts to #2963 and #2958. #2962 owns the cost rollup (`get_spend`, `cost.price_entries`, `cost.run_totals`, `get_run_cost`) because #2963's kept half reads the rollup rather than builds it and its reconciliation half is cut; #2970 owns the auto-approval rules (`set_approval_rules`, `get_auto_eligibility`) because #2958's kept scope is the registry, connections, grants and kill switches. Unresolved, the approval card ships with no eligibility line and Run ships with no Cost tab. Applied to §1.1, §1.2 and §8.
- **Billing: this document is the page's only design.** `pBilling` (`mc.html:9266-9330`) still draws spec §12.1's superseded per-run model — 1,000 free runs a month, $0.30 per run, a retained-evidence tile, "Governed actions — reported, not priced" — and there is no GAU bucket, billing mode, auto top-up, contracted rate or purchase form anywhere in `mc.html`. §1.4 and §3.9 are the design; WL-38, WL-44 and WL-45 take nothing from `pBilling` or from `pages/billing.md`. The same superseded "first 1,000 runs a month free" string survives in the mockup's Billing empty state, its onboarding lead, tags and offer card and the money-asked scenario note, and is corrected in the Mockups repo, not here. The layout half of this note is superseded on 2026-09-18: `pages/billing.md` now describes the GAU model and the page follows its arrangement (see "The `pBilling` exception, closed").
- **`dispatch_tacho_command` keeps its name until WL-50.** Spec Appendix E renames it `dispatch_command` under ADR-025; this document binds the registered name in four places (§1.2 Run row, §1.3, §1.5, INV-28). WL-50 carries the rename as a residue item so the contract, handler, API route, MCP tool, doc and the four bindings move in one PR. A lane that writes `dispatch_command` before then will not resolve in the registry.
- **W13 "In the loop" is in this release (ADR-090, 2026-09-18).** Mockups `origin/main` carries `w13-in-the-loop.html` and `docs/w13-in-the-loop-scenario.md`, which introduce skills resolution as a governed door (`search_skills` over `.oxagen/skills.toml`), `control.interject` / `control.answer`, a quarantined reflection capture and workspace creation mid-run — seven vocabulary terms, and eight frame kinds once ADR-090 settled that the reflection is a post-seal record rather than the ninth frame W13 drew. This bullet previously read "out of this release", on the reasoning that `CLAUDE.md` states there is no skill system in this repo and spec §2 states Oxagen has no skills engine. That reasoning read "no skills engine" as "no skills surface", and those are different claims: running a skill and resolving one are separate acts, and ADR-043 cut only the first. **Reversed, with the 2026-09-15 narrowing it carried.** Oxagen still runs no skill; it governs resolution, the same shape as the toolbelt in §4.4. ADR-090 carries the decision, the ten rules it settles and the alternatives rejected; spec §2 gains the run-versus-resolve sentence and keeps its "no skills engine" line, and §7.5's gateway-kind table, §14, Appendix A (§A.10 `skills`), Appendix E and §19's page-coverage table gain their Skills and W13 rows. Appendix C is untouched: it is a single envelope example and enumerates no kinds. Appendix F is deliberately not reconciled and no longer claims complete route coverage. The #3098 lane builds it and depends on #3241 for the repository binding.
- **Spec divergences a lane will hit, recorded here because this document wins (Status block).** The spec's §0 row 14, §14 and Appendix F still claim nine pages and carry a full Audit row (§0 item 1 ships eight, Audit cut). §14's and Appendix F's Billing rows still read "plan, run allowance, meters, invoices" and "Governed-action usage, the retention meter, plan, and invoices", both superseded by the ADR-055 amendment that sits in §12.1 and was never carried across. Appendix E's amendment count line reads 102 where the families sum to 93 (Repositories is 3 rows not 13; Identity-and-access is 11 rows not 10 and its header still says 10), and `authorize_cli` has no row although §0 item 8 ships it, WL-21 builds it and spec §14.1 describes `oxagen login`. Appendix F's prose still sends the Ontology and Audit rows to pages that do not ship and omits `/{org}/billing/governed-actions` and `/{org}/billing/usage`; §7.3 holds the correct table and `proxy.test.ts` asserts against §7.3.
- **Not changed: the shell's assistant flyout.** The Mockups branch `small-approvals-bottom-dock-scenarios` moves the in-app agent from the sidebar flyout to a bottom dock and makes approvals small by default, but its staged `mc.html` is older than `origin/main` (it renames the tenant back from `a-intel` to `acme`, drops the PRODUCT gate on Scenarios and removes the `obFirstRun` logic Fleet reads) and would revert the tenant rename, the PRODUCT/mobile split and the Ontology removal if committed as-is. `origin/main` is the design of record, so the §1.2 shell row keeps the assistant flyout per ADR-053 and the #2968 lane note is unchanged. If the bottom dock is adopted, it lands on a rebase onto `origin/main` and the shell row and the #2968 lane note change with it. Maintainer decision 9 of 2026-09-15: not adopted.

**2026-09-14 — Scale-back: the Audit page and the year-two features leave this release; the in-app agent stays; witness moves first.** Source: the product review of 2026-09-14 ("The scale-back question", `oxagen-mission-control-review.html`), which the maintainer adopted with one exception: "dont cut the in app agent". The review's call: keep Fleet, Run, Spend and the toolbelt (they work on day one with no configuration); thin Tools, Billing, Organization and Steering to what a design partner asks for now; defer Audit to the write-once archive at seal time ("nearly free and the chain depends on it") and cut legal holds, crypto-shredding and reconciliation; cut Ontology (already applied above); move the witness from the end of the roadmap to the front ("the only one that makes an outside party depend on your records"). The review also cut the in-app agent ("a chat assistant that sells nothing"); the maintainer kept it. Applied — **cut**, with no page, route, sidebar link, `UNRECORDED` row or placeholder: the Audit page (#2966 closed; the recorder keeps the archive at seal; legacy `/security`, `/access`, `/governance` and `/audit` redirect to `/{org}`), Run › Context (#2954 closed), Tools › policy simulation, Cedar versions and the assurance suite (#2959 closed), Spend › reconciliation, agent scores and percentiles (#2969 closed), SSO/SCIM, model funding routes and data-plane UI in Organization, two-person mandates, steering effect, retirement and promotion thresholds. **Kept**, built as gap lanes (§8): Fleet; Run (recorder and replay #2952, controls #2953, proof/witness #2955 — first in the sequence); Agents (#2956, mandates #2957: identity, credential broker, roles, toolbelt, mandates); Tools (#2958: registry, connections, kill switches, auto-approval rules); Steering (#2961: records → proposals → Context PR); Spend (#2963: attribution and findings); Organization (#2964: people, roles, invitations, workspaces, API keys); Billing as designed; the onboarding gate (#2967); shell notifications and ⌘K and the assistant flyout on `stella-serve` per ADR-053 (#2968) — WL-06 deletes the fixture-backed flyout and the lane rebuilds it on live backing, so the architecture lists the assistant as a rev1 shell feature built by the #2968 lane, not as cut. Thinned issues carry a "Scope note" section: #2955, #2957, #2958, #2961, #2963, #2964, #2968. Applied here: the status table (Inputs); §0 item 1; §1.1; §1.2 (no Audit row; the shell row; the Agents, Tools, Steering, Spend and Run rows say what ships; 19 `page.tsx` files, 3 org pages; `/{org}/audit/**` deleted outright); §3.6 and INV-18 (`UNRECORDED` has five keys at WL-33 and each lane deletes its page's key); §6.3 (19 steps); §7.1 (the shell-trim row is not a cut; `[org]/audit/**` deleted); §7.3 (the Audit rows retarget to `/{org}`); §8 (Gap lanes); and `architecture.worklist.json` (the rev1 scope rows, the Not-recorded seam, INV-18, the page-load journey, WL-06, WL-08, WL-32, WL-33, WL-47, WL-49, and the openQuestions row on the cut). The placeholder route `src/app/[org]/audit/[[...tab]]/page.tsx` on `app-rebuild` is deleted in the same PR with its two `baseline.json` rows; the sidebar's `audit` key, its label and its ⌘K command are WL-32's job (as the Ontology entries were) and stay until that lane.

**2026-09-14 — Ontology leaves rev1 scope.** Maintainer decision: "remove ontology from the things we build it wont ship" (#2960, closed as not planned). The `ontology.*` / `graph.*` capabilities stay on the API and MCP; the app gets no Ontology page. Applied: §0 and §1.2 (no Ontology row; sidebar 9 links; 20 `page.tsx` files, 6 workspace pages), §3.6 and INV-18 (`UNRECORDED` has six keys, `ontology` dropped), §6.3 (20 steps), §7.1 (`[ws]/ontology/**` deleted outright, not converted to `NotRecorded`), §7.3 (legacy `knowledge/**` other than `knowledge/memory`, and `settings/github`, redirect to Fleet), and `architecture.worklist.json` (WL-32, WL-33, WL-47, WL-49, the rev1 scope and seam rows).

### 9.-2 Revision-5 objections (this revision)

Two objections against revision 5 were verified at `27b9d2520` and are applied here; neither changes the model, the statuses or the worklist's ids.

| # | Objection | Resolution |
|---|---|---|
| R5-1 | `createGauInvoice` created the Stripe invoice with `auto_advance: true` (§3.9 item 11, WL-31, the provider seam in `architecture.worklist.json`). Stripe finalizes such an invoice on its own one hour after creation and then collects it on its schedule (https://docs.stripe.com/invoicing/integration/automatic-advancement-collection), so a draft Oxagen abandoned — a crash between `invoices.create` and `finalizeInvoice`, or a `pending` auto top-up the resume step marked `failed` as `superseded` after a Checkout landed — was still finalized and charged by Stripe with no settlement row that would ever grant it, and the `failed` status ("no collectable invoice exists for this row") was false for exactly those rows. | Accepted. `invoices.create` runs with `auto_advance: false` and `invoices.finalizeInvoice(invoiceId, { auto_advance: true })` turns collection on only for an invoice Oxagen finalized (item 11), so Stripe's collection and retry schedule never apply to a draft Oxagen did not finalize. The resume step's superseded branch, when `stripe_invoice_id` is set, calls the new provider method `deleteOrVoidDraftInvoice({ settlementId, invoiceId })` — `invoices.del` for a `draft`, `invoices.voidInvoice` when Stripe reports it `open`, `settleGauPaid` when it reports `paid` — before `settleGauFailed` (item 10); the method joins the provider seam list (§3) and the provider paragraph. Item 7 is restated: a `failed` row means Oxagen holds no invoice or deleted its draft. Tests added to WL-31 and §6.1: a provider test asserting `auto_advance: false` on create and `auto_advance: true` on finalize, and a job test asserting the delete call for a superseded row with an invoice id. |
| R5-2 | WL-21 moves `assertOrgRole` and `resolveActorOrgRole` into `packages/iam/src/org-role.ts` and has `packages/handlers/src/lib/api-key-authz.ts` re-export them, but `packages/handlers/package.json` does not depend on `@oxagen/iam` and the workspace hoists nothing (no `hoist-pattern`, `public-hoist-pattern` or `shamefully-hoist` in `.npmrc` or `pnpm-workspace.yaml`), so the re-export fails to resolve. The worklist also let WL-11's classifier and WL-21's handler throw and match `HandlerError` before WL-22 introduced the class, and let the billing handler items (WL-25–WL-30) call `assertOrgRole` and `HandlerError` with no edge to WL-21 or WL-22. | Accepted. WL-21 gains `packages/handlers/package.json` and `pnpm-lock.yaml` in its paths, `"@oxagen/iam": "workspace:*"` and `pnpm i --no-frozen-lockfile` in its acceptance; this creates no cycle (`@oxagen/iam` depends on `@oxagen/billing`, `@oxagen/database`, `@oxagen/oxagen`, `@oxagen/run-evidence`, `@oxagen/telemetry`, none of which depends on `@oxagen/handlers`). `dependsOn` edges added: WL-22 → WL-11 and WL-21; WL-21 and WL-22 → WL-25, WL-26, WL-27, WL-28, WL-29, WL-30 (each acceptance calls `assertOrgRole` or throws `HandlerError`); WL-21 → WL-14, whose acceptance now names the `assertOrgRole` call on `resolve_approval` that §1.2, §3.2 and WL-40 already attributed to it (the handler has no role check today, `agent.approval.resolve.ts`). The app-side items that match `HandlerError` or describe the check (WL-40, WL-42, WL-45) reach WL-21 and WL-22 through the items they already depend on. The graph stays acyclic (checked by a topological sort over every item). §8's phase sentence names the new waits. |

Beyond the above, this revision changed: §3 (the seam list); §3.2 (the roles paragraph); §3.9 items 7, 10 and 11, the provider paragraph; §6.1 (a Provider row, the Recorder and Close job rows); §8; and `architecture.worklist.json` (the provider and recorder seam contracts; WL-11, WL-14, WL-21 and WL-25–WL-30 `dependsOn`; WL-14 acceptance; WL-21 paths and acceptance; WL-31 title and acceptance; openQuestions on settlement states).

### 9.-1 Revision-4 objections (revision 5)

Three verifiers reviewed revision 4. "Accepted" means the objection was right on the evidence, re-verified at `27b9d2520`; "Rebutted" and "Accepted in part" carry the evidence. The three lenses raised the same three defects in the settlement design (the guard, the card-less org, the annual bucket); each is listed once under the lens that stated it first and cross-referenced.

| # | Objection | Resolution |
|---|---|---|
| R4-A1 (= R4-M1, R4-I2) | `settleGauPaid` guarded on `status = 'pending'` while a declined `pay` set the row `failed`, so Stripe's successful retry (`invoice.paid`) matched no row: the customer was charged and nothing was granted, the episode never cleared, `pastDue` never cleared. A late payment after rollover would also have credited a closed bucket. `settleGauFailed(id, reason)` never recorded the invoice id. | Accepted. Four statuses with `paid` the only terminal one (§3.9 item 7): `open` is "Stripe holds a finalized, unpaid invoice"; `settleGauPaid(tx, id)` is `WHERE id = :id AND status <> 'paid'` and grants exactly once, through `ensureCurrentBucket` to the org's current bucket, clearing `open_topup_settlement_id` wherever it points; `settleGauOpen` and `settleGauFailed` are `WHERE status = 'pending'`; `recordGauInvoice` persists `stripe_invoice_id` as soon as `invoices.create` returns (item 8c). INV-30 and the WL-31 tests are restated: paid twice grants once; `open` then paid grants once; `failed` then paid grants; paid on a `paid` row changes nothing; paid after rollover lands in the current bucket. `invoice.payment_failed` routes to `settleGauOpen`, never to `failed`. |
| R4-A2 (= R4-M3, R4-I-n4) | An invoice-billed org has no rev1 path to a saved card, `invoices.pay` on a customer with no payment source is an `invalid_request_error` (`stripe-provider.ts:552-558` throws for the same condition today), the design classified every non-card error as a throw, so every interim and period-close settlement of such an org stayed `pending` with no invoice id, replayed the same keys (Stripe returns the stored result), and after 24 h was marked `failed` while a finalized invoice existed in Stripe with no settlement id; `list_invoices` would have labelled it `subscription`. | Accepted. The provider path is two calls with the invoice id persisted between them (`createGauInvoice`, `recordGauInvoice`, `finalizeAndPayGauInvoice`, item 11); `collection` is chosen from the org's default payment method — `charge_automatically` with `default_payment_method`, else `send_invoice` with `days_until_due: 30` — so `pay` is never called on a customer without a source, and every post-finalization outcome that is not `paid` (card error, SCA, no source, `send_invoice`) is `open`; the resume step replays keys only for rows with no invoice id and younger than 24 h (the only rows a replay can help) and resumes rows with an id from Stripe's state. The no-card case is in openQuestions (the 30-day term; no card form for invoice-billed orgs in rev1, `createPaymentMethodSetupIntent` at `payment-methods.ts:121` being the helper a later lane would use). Tests added (§6.1 recorder row). |
| R4-A3 (= R4-M2) | The bucket period followed the subscription interval, so a `year` subscriber got a twelve-month allowance and one overage invoice a year, against the amendment's "monthly included GAU allowance, billed in advance each month" and "a monthly invoice at period end". | Accepted. Every bucket is one month (§3.9 item 4): the anniversary-day slice of the current cycle for a subscriber (`periodFor`, `addMonths` clamping as Stripe's anchor does; a `month` plan's slice is `current_period_start/end`), the UTC calendar month otherwise; `included_gau = included_gau_per_month`; `months_in_period` is gone from item 4, ADR-055, WL-23 and WL-25; the close job closes each org's month; A′4's log entry records the supersession. |
| R4-I1 | WL-24 and WL-27 wrote migrations into `packages/database/drizzle/`, the retired archive (`drizzle/README.md:1-10`: "Nothing in this folder runs"); CI applies `packages/database/atlas/migrations/` (`pipeline.yml:302,469,563`, `atlas.hcl:38`), so no CI job would have had the tables; `manifest-coverage.test.ts:36-74` fails on any `org_id` table missing from `tenant-policy.manifest.ts`; `schema:manifest:check` (`pipeline.yml:175`) fails on `storage-manifest.json` drift; `tenant-policy.manifest.ts:99` keeps the counters entry. | Accepted. WL-24 and WL-27 name timestamped files under `packages/database/atlas/migrations/` (generated by `pnpm db:migrate:diff` from the drizzle schema, then reviewed), `atlas.sum` re-stamped with `atlas migrate hash`, the three tables added to `tenant-policy.manifest.ts` as `org_only` with the policy DDL from `tools/scripts/gen-rls-migration.ts`, the counters entry removed in WL-27, `storage-manifest.json` regenerated in both, and `pnpm db:lint-migrations`, `db:atlas-validate`, `schema:manifest:check` and `pnpm --filter @oxagen/database test:integration` in both acceptances. |
| R4-I3 | `resolve_approval` returned `{ resolution: "expired" }` as a success when its `UPDATE` matched no row (`agent.approval.resolve.ts:39-47`), and the kernel accrues one GAU on every successful top-level non-`noBillingGate` invoke (`kernel.ts:1543-1580`, `governedActionUnits` → 1), so a no-op was billed and, for a prepaid org at `remaining ≤ 0`, ran the auto top-up charge. | Accepted. The handler throws `HandlerError { code: "conflict", reason: "approval_expired" }` when `updated.length === 0`; `expired` leaves the output enum; WL-14 depends on WL-22 for `HandlerError`; the handler test asserts no debit through a fake recorder; WL-40's `conflict` comes from the §3.2 `HandlerError` row (§3.9 item 15, §1.5). |
| R4-I4 | INV-29's arch test ("every handler for a contract with `defaultRoles` calls `assertOrgRole`") could not go green: 232 of 240 contract files declare `defaultRoles`, 6 handlers check a role. | Accepted. The test is over a named array — the nine INV-29 contracts plus the four that already check — growing per lane; the invariant sentence names the same set (INV-29). |
| R4-I5 | The bucket writers were specified with `incrementActionCounter`'s `withTenantDb` shape ("the caller must be inside a tenant scope", `action-metering.ts:305-317`) and called from the webhooks (`withSystemDb` throughout, `webhooks.ts:1-4,51,90,109,250`) and the Inngest job, which have no tenant scope; `grants.ts:150-162` documents the resulting `TenantScopeError`. A paid Checkout would have thrown at the grant. | Accepted. Every writer takes its executor first (`ensureCurrentBucket(tx, …)`, the claims, `recordGauInvoice`, the three settles), the `tryInsertGrantLedger(tx, …)` shape; the recorder passes a `withTenantDb` transaction, the webhook branches and the job a `withSystemDb` one with the `// tenancy: system bypass` comment (§3.9 item 8); WL-28 and WL-31 test both branches with no active scope under `TENANT_RLS_ENFORCEMENT_ENABLED`. |
| R4-A-n1 (= R4-I-n1) | `BillingInvoice` has no metadata field (`provider.ts:210-233`; `stripeInvoiceToNeutral` keeps `orgId` and `billingReason`), yet the dispatch routed on `invoice.metadata.gau_settlement_id`. | Accepted. `gauSettlementId: string \| null` on `BillingInvoice`, read beside `resolveInvoiceOrgId` (provider paragraph; WL-31). |
| R4-A-n2 | `get_gau_bucket` and the gate read the mode through `getOrgBillingSettings`, an `INSERT … ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` on every call (`billing-settings.ts:97-115`), while both acceptances assert no INSERT. | Accepted. `readOrgBillingSettings` (a `SELECT` returning column defaults for a missing row) for the whole GAU path; the row is written only by `set_auto_topup`, `set_org_billing_terms` and `ensureStripeCustomer` (§3.9 item 6, WL-25). |
| R4-A-n3 | INV-31's string scan failed on the three tests that spell `platformOperator` to assert its absence. | Accepted. `*.test.ts` excluded from the walk (INV-31). |
| R4-A-n4 | WL-50 said `create_api_key`, `revoke_api_key`, `change_member_role` and `remove_org_member` gain `app` in `layers`; all four already declare it (`api.key.create.ts:23`, `api.key.revoke.ts:20`, `org.member_role.change.ts:19`, `org.member.remove.ts:21`) and are bound in `apps/app_deprecated/capability-ui-map.json`. | Accepted. WL-50 lists them as carried over and keeps their deprecated-map bindings until `APP_DIR` flips. |
| R4-A-n5 | `OXAGEN_ACTION_METER_MODE` shadow mode (`action-metering.ts:260-264`, `env.ts:334`, `registry.ts:763`, `.env.example:194`) was not mentioned. | Accepted. Deleted with its resolver in WL-25: a shadow that charged a card would not be a shadow, and one that did not would leave a prepaid org running past exhaustion unrecorded (§3.9 item 13, §7.1). |
| R4-A-n6 (= R4-M-n4) | The virtual bucket set `remaining = included` and ignored the previous month's carry, so a prepaid org with `included_gau_per_month = 0` and carried purchased units was refused at the first action of a new month. | Accepted. `readBucket` applies the item 5 carry formula to the virtual bucket; `get_gau_bucket` prints it (§3.9 items 5 and 9). |
| R4-A-n7 | `getCheckoutPaymentMethod` expanded `payment_intent`, under which `payment_method` is a string id. | Accepted. `expand: ["payment_intent.payment_method"]` (provider paragraph, WL-28). |
| R4-A-n8 | INV-30 read "never an invoice-billed one" while item 9 refuses a suspended org before the mode check. | Accepted. INV-30 reworded. |
| R4-A-n9 | A switch from invoice billing to prepaid mid-month stranded uninvoiced overage; `set_org_billing_terms` is unscoped and its upsert needed `withSystemDb`. | Accepted. The handler runs on `withSystemDb` and closes the accrual at the switch with `claimInterimInvoice(tx, bucketId, uninvoiced)` — the same function as the threshold crossing, with an explicit quantity (§3.9 items 8d and 12; §6.1 row). |
| R4-A-n10 | `ensureStripeCustomer` reads through `withTenantDb` (`customers.ts:19`), which the job cannot enter. | Accepted. `ensureStripeCustomer(orgId, { system: true })`, the `opts.system` switch `getOrgBillingSettings` already has (`billing-settings.ts:80-96`); the job and the operator handler pass it (§3.9 item 10). |
| R4-M-n1 | `platformOperator: true` is a plain caller-supplied boolean, the class the kernel refuses as a forged binding for `deployedAgentInvocation` (`types.ts:442-453`, `kernel.ts:908-948`). | Accepted, although non-blocking: `createPlatformOperatorContext` mints a binding registered in a module-private `WeakSet`; a literal `true` or a copied object is refused as a forged binding with a security event; the string scan stays as the second mechanism (§3.9 item 12, INV-31). |
| R4-M-n2 | `gau_settlements.amount_cents`, `failure_reason` and the `(org_id, created_at)` index had no named reader. | Accepted. All three dropped: the amount and hosted URL come from the `billing.invoices` mirror, the failure reason is the job's log line, and every reader reaches a settlement through its bucket (§3.9 item 7). |
| R4-M-n3 | Items 8c/8d did not say the claim transaction commits before the provider call. | Accepted. Stated, with the consequence (a crash after the commit leaves a `pending` row the job can resume) and a query-log-order assertion (§3.9 item 8c, §6.1). |
| R4-M-n5 | A daily 00:10 retry turned a Stripe failure on the 17th into an invoice on the 18th. | Accepted. The job runs hourly; both steps were already idempotent (§3.9 item 10). |
| R4-M-n6 (= R4-I-n5) | The amendment's verbatim fires at "gau # 100,001" on the total; the adopted model and item 8d count uninvoiced overage beyond the allowance at `≥ invoice_gau_max`. | Accepted as an openQuestion: the design follows the model table ("reaches `invoice_gau_max`", uninvoiced overage `≥ max`), and openQuestions asks which count the cap bounds and whether the boundary is `≥ max` or `> max`. |
| R4-M-n7 | Root `package.json:83` `playwright` has no importer and no CI use. | Accepted. Dropped in WL-02 (§7.1). |
| R4-M-n8 (= R4-I-n10) | `.gitignore:41` is `**/e2e/screenshots/` and no `MC_DATA` line exists; `nightly.yml:15` is `GIT_CONFIG_COUNT`, `E2E_TEST: "1"` is at `:309`. | Accepted. §5, §6.3, §7.1, WL-04 and WL-46 corrected. |
| R4-M-n9 (= R4-I-n9) | Announcer prose at R3-I1 and the X-not-Y contrast at §3.1; WL-23 still said "tokens stay reported at zero". | Accepted. Both sentences restated; WL-23 says tokens are not on the page. |
| R4-M-n10 | `get_run`'s 20 s long poll runs inside the kernel invoke; the stream route's `maxDuration` was unstated. | Accepted. The IAM query and the audit and security emissions are per invoke by construction (the wait is inside the handler, after the gates and before the output validates, `kernel.ts:1313-1316,1543-1549`); WL-35 exports `maxDuration` on the stream `route.ts` to cover `idleMs` and names the platform limit as the bound (§3.5). |
| R4-I-n2 | WL-27 removed `includedActionsAnnual` from `resolveOrgActionEntitlement` without listing its readers (`billing.action_rate_card.ts:59,66,85`, `billing.action_estimate.ts:65-110`, `bootstrap.ts:48`). | Accepted in part. The field stays; after the column drop it is `included_gau_per_month × 12` from the plan row, one line in `plan-allowance.ts:87-90`, so the three readers and `get_rate_card`/`preview_action_cost` are untouched (§3.9 contracts table, WL-27). |
| R4-I-n3 | `pnpm check:audit-coverage` (`audit-coverage.test.ts:96-137`) requires every `packages/handlers/src/billing.*.ts` file to call `emitSecurityEvent(` or carry `// audit-exempt:`. | Accepted. The three mutations emit, the three reads carry the comment (§3.9 after the contracts table; WL-26–WL-30). |
| R4-I-n6 | INV-02 named `typescript` 6.0.3 and `@typescript/native-preview` 7.0.0-dev; `apps/app`'s `tsc` is `@typescript/native` = `typescript@7.0.2` and its `typescript` alias is `@typescript/typescript6@6.0.2` (`apps/app/package.json:15,65,75`, `pnpm-workspace.yaml:16-19`). | Accepted. INV-02 names the actual pair. |
| R4-I-n7 | Item 8c did not say where `customerId` and `paymentMethodId` come from, and the recorder should never call `ensureStripeCustomer`. | Accepted in part. Auto top-up reads `org_billing_settings.stripe_customer_id` (non-null whenever a default card exists, since only a Checkout saves one) and the default `billing.payment_methods` row, with no customer creation. The interim path is the exception: an invoice-billed org can reach `invoice_gau_max` without ever having bought anything, so no customer exists; the recorder calls `ensureStripeCustomer(orgId)` inside its tenant scope, which returns the stored id when set and creates the customer once otherwise (§3.9 items 8c and 8d). |
| R4-I-n8 | A paid Checkout cleared the episode while an auto top-up row could still be `pending` (Stripe unreachable at claim time); the retry would then charge it too. | Accepted. The resume step settles a `pending` `auto_topup` row whose bucket has `remaining > 0` as `failed` (`superseded`), with no provider call (§3.9 item 10). |
| R4-I-n9 | WL-30's `surface: 'runner'` needed to be the `CapabilityContext.surface` field, not `opts.surface`, which `surfaces: []` refuses as `surface_denied` (`kernel.ts:993`). | Accepted. Stated in item 12 and WL-30. |
| R4-I-n10 | `gau-settlements.pg.test.ts` in `test:unit` would be red locally without Postgres "as the other DB-backed unit tests in the package do". | Accepted in substance; the cited convention is elsewhere: `packages/billing` has no Postgres-backed unit test today, and the repo's gate for a store-backed test in a unit run is `describe.skipIf(!present)` in `packages/telemetry`'s `*.integration.test.ts` files. The file uses `describe.skipIf(!process.env.DATABASE_URL)`; CI's `test` job migrates Postgres before `turbo run build test:unit` and carries `DATABASE_URL` in `globalEnv` (§6.1). |

Beyond the above, revision 5 changed: the status table and §0 item 9; §1.4 (`pastDue` and `lastAttempt` wording); §1.5 (`resolve_approval`'s `conflict`); §3.9 items 4–12 and 15, the contracts table, the audit-coverage sentence, the provider and webhook paragraphs; INV-02, INV-29, INV-30, INV-31; §5, §6.1, §6.3, §7.1, §8; and design.json (WL-02, WL-14, WL-22 path, WL-23, WL-24, WL-25, WL-27, WL-28, WL-29, WL-30, WL-31, WL-35, WL-40, WL-46, WL-50; INV-02, INV-29, INV-30, INV-31; openQuestions 1, 3, 4 and three new entries).

### 9.0 Revision-3 objections (revision 4)

Three verifiers reviewed revision 3. "Accepted" means the objection was right on the evidence, re-verified at `27b9d2520`; "Rebutted" carries the evidence. The billing amendment of 2026-09-13 (§3.9) arrived in the same round and is adopted exactly; it supersedes the exhaustion and top-up parts of revision 3's §3.9, §1.4 and the ADR-055 draft, and removes the "`hard_stop` only" and "auto top-up is future" open questions.

| # | Objection | Resolution |
|---|---|---|
| R3-A1 | The pre-tenant reads for `/` and `/cli/authorize` have no port: `shell.context` takes an `OrgCtx`, the landing runs under `PretenantCtx`, and `list_workspaces` needs an `orgSlug` the landing only knows after `list_orgs` returns. | Accepted. `DataSource.pretenant = { orgs(ctx: PretenantCtx), workspaces(ctx: PretenantCtx, orgSlug) }` in `ports.ts`, implemented in `src/data/live/pretenant.ts` on the `PretenantCtx` overload, with exactly two callers — `features/shell/landing.ts` (WL-32) and `features/auth/cli-consent.ts` (WL-12) — so INV-17 holds; §1.2, §3.3, §3.7, WL-12, WL-32 and design.json describe the same call. `shell.context` stays on `OrgCtx` for the switchers. |
| R3-A2 | `Read<T>` carries an `exhausted` variant no read can produce (every app read is `noBillingGate`; the kernel skips both gates, `kernel.ts:1313-1316,1328-1334`). | Accepted. `Read<T>` has no `exhausted` variant (§3.3); the §3.2 table's Read column says so; the `kernelRead` negatives for `gau_exhausted`/`budget_exceeded` and the `exhausted` entry in the component-state list are gone; the INV-28 positive (a `noBillingGate` read succeeds at `remaining = 0`) stays. `ActionResult` keeps `exhausted` for `resolve_approval`. |
| R3-A3 | The retention slice renders a constant: `get_evidence_retention` returns `storedGbBeyondIncluded: null, storedGbMeasured: false` unconditionally (`billing.evidence_retention.ts:118-125`). | Accepted. `billing.retention`, `RetentionMeter`, its mapper, component and tests, the retention half of "Reported, not billed", and `get_evidence_retention` in INV-25/INV-27/WL-38/WL-50 are removed (§1.4). The contract is untouched for its API and MCP callers. `chargeEvidenceRetention` and `retentionCreditsForGbMonths`, the dead charger, are deleted in WL-25 (§3.9 item 13). |
| R3-A4 | WL-16's "writes nothing billing-shaped" cannot pass: `organizationCreateHandler` calls `grantFreeCredits` (`org.create.ts:11,169-195` → `grants.ts:150-162`). | Accepted. WL-16 deletes the call and the import; §7.1 lists them; ADR-055 states the ADR-053 assistant balance of a new org starts at zero; whether a signup grant returns with the assistant is an openQuestion. The e2e seed inherits the deletion. |
| R3-A5 | `start_subscription_upgrade` gains `noBillingGate` and an INV-27 test although no rev1 app path invokes it. | Accepted. Dropped from INV-27, WL-25 and the contracts table; the contract is unchanged. |
| R3-A6 | INV-20's file-set equality omits `e2e/.gitignore` and the runtime `e2e/.auth/owner.json`. | Accepted. The arch test ignores entries starting with `.`; INV-20 names them. |
| R3-A7 | `nightly.yml:344` runs `pnpm test:e2e` under `E2E_TEST: "1"` and no Stripe key, and is in no worklist path. | Accepted. WL-46 sets `E2E_TEST: "true"` there; WL-48 adds the same fail-closed `Stripe test key present` step (§6.3). |
| R3-A8 | `pay.spec.ts` hardcodes `metadata.gau_quantity === "2000"`, baking in a block size openQuestions leaves to the maintainer. | Accepted. The rate block carries `data-block-size`; the spec asserts `String(2 × blockSize)` (§1.4, §6.3). |
| R3-A9 | The stated reason for `org_billing_settings.stripe_customer_id` (a Free org mints a customer per purchase) is not what `ensureStripeCustomer` does: `customers.ts:38-45` searches by metadata before creating. | Accepted. The column is kept for the actual reason — the search is eventually consistent, so two quick purchases race — and the write is an upsert on `org_billing_settings` keyed on `org_id`, tested against an org with no row (§3.9, provider paragraph). |
| R3-A10 | `get_subscription` returns `subscription: null` for the e2e org; the Plan card's mapper is unspecified for that case. | Accepted. §1.4: the card prints "No subscription" and nothing else; the tier comes from `get_contract_rate.tier`. |
| R3-A11 | Columns with no reader (`gau_buckets.terms_source`/`terms_ref`, `gau_purchases.granted_bucket_id`) and a status with no producer (`expired`). | Accepted. `terms_source`/`terms_ref` are dropped (terms are resolved live; each settlement records its rate). `gau_purchases` is replaced by `gau_settlements` (§3.9 item 7), whose `bucket_id` is read by the claim statements and the close job and whose `stripe_invoice_id` is read by `list_invoices`; the webhook inserts the `checkout` row already `paid`, so no pending insert and no `expired` status exist. |
| R3-A12 | The token half of "Reported, not billed" is product surface with no billing consequence, and `periodUsage` is null without an active subscription. | Accepted. Off the page (§1.4). |
| R3-A13 | `routes.org(slug)` and `routes.people(org)` both build `/{org}`; `ContractInput<C>` has no consumer. | Accepted. `people(org)` only (§3.8); `ContractInput` is gone (§3.2). |
| R3-A14 | INV-10 names `grade` and `verdict`, which no rev1 view model carries. | Accepted. Scoped to `basis` and `tier` (INV-10). |
| R3-A15 | `/invite/[token]` is public (`proxy.ts:22-24`); the anonymous found-state is unlisted. | Accepted. §1.2 lists `resolveInvitee`'s five outcomes, the anonymous-found state included, as component tests. |
| R3-I1 | INV-02's mechanism does not compile and does not close `Object.assign`: a subclass of a class with a private constructor is TS2675; `Object.assign<T,U,V>` returns `T & U & V`, assignable to `OrgCtx`, so the `@ts-expect-error` is unused (TS2578); nothing at runtime refuses the copy. | Accepted. The brand is an ES `#brand` private field on each class, `declare`d fields, a protected base constructor, private leaf constructors, a `MINT` token only `viewer-mint.ts` exports, and `static is(x): x is OrgCtx { return #brand in x }`; the shared kernel core refuses any ctx that fails `is` with `error("invalid_ctx", 500)` before building the tenant scope (§3.1, §3.2 step 0, INV-02). Verified in this round with a probe of the exact §3.1 code: `tsc` 6.0.3 (`node_modules/typescript`) and `@typescript/native-preview` 7.0.0-dev.20260707.2 both compile `viewer.type-test.ts` with all six `@ts-expect-error` directives consumed (removing the spread directive surfaces TS2741 "Property '#brand' is missing" under both); under Node 24 the emitted classes give `OrgCtx.is` false for a plain object, an `Object.assign({}, real, …)` copy, a spread copy, `structuredClone(real)` and `Object.create(OrgCtx.prototype)`, `Object.assign(real, { orgId })` throws `TypeError`, and a forged token throws. The probe also showed that `Object.freeze` returns `Readonly<T>`, which drops `#brand` from the type, so `mint` freezes and returns the same reference; and that freezing inside the base constructor makes a subclass's own class-field definitions throw `TypeError: Cannot redefine property` under `useDefineForClassFields`, so every field is `declare`d. The `Object.assign({}, …)` type test is removed; a `no-restricted-syntax` ban on `Object.assign(` and `structuredClone(` under `src/` outside `viewer.ts`/`viewer.testing.ts` has a probe each. |
| R3-I2 | `invoice.paid` also reaches `onInvoiceRecovered`, which resets dunning to `active` for any paid invoice it can bind to an org (`dunning.ts:280-330`); after WL-28 a suspended org buying one GAU block is reset by a payment that did not settle the overdue subscription invoice. `onInvoicePaymentFailed` has the same gap. | Accepted. Both are guarded to `invoice.subscriptionId !== null` in the webhook dispatch; GAU invoices route to `settleGauPaid`/`settleGauFailed` by `metadata.gau_settlement_id`; a webhook test covers the suspended-org GAU payment, the subscription recovery, and a declined GAU invoice not entering grace (§3.9, webhook paragraph; WL-28, WL-31). |
| R3-I3 | §3.2 step 4 wrapped `runInTenantScope` around `invoke()`, which already enters the scope (`kernel.ts:1064-1081`). | Accepted. The core only fills the `CapabilityContext`; the kernel owns scope entry; `@oxagen/tenancy` is not imported under `src/` (§3.2 step 4, INV-03). |
| R3-I4 | `ActionResult.field` has no stated source. | Accepted. `kernelWrite` pre-parses with the contract's own schema and takes `field` from the first issue path; a kernel `invalid_input` after a successful pre-parse is `invalid` with no field (§3.2 step 5). |
| R3-I5 | `kernelWrite`'s first overload admits `PretenantCtx` on any contract. | Accepted. `kernelWrite(ctx: PretenantCtx, contract: UnscopedContract)` mirrors the read path (§3.2). |
| R3-I6 | `get_gau_bucket` and `get_contract_rate` both return `blockSizeGau`; two readers of one figure can disagree. | Accepted. Dropped from `get_gau_bucket`; the rate block owns it (§3.9 contracts table). |
| R3-I7 | `ensureStripeCustomer` reason misstated. | Accepted (as R3-A9). |
| R3-I8 | `resolveContractTerms` falls back to the Free plan while `resolveOrgActionEntitlement` and `resolveOrgTier` honour `organizations.plan_type`. | Accepted. `resolveContractTerms` does not read `plan_type` and ADR-055 says so; `resolveOrgTier` keeps its leg for the IAM tier gate (§3.9 item 3). |
| R3-I9 | `gau_purchases.stripe_invoice_id` is "set on grant" but `BillingCheckoutSession` carries no invoice field (`provider.ts:341-349`). | Accepted. The normalised session gains `invoiceId: string \| null` from `sess.invoice`; WL-28 (§3.9, provider paragraph). |
| R3-I10 | The `gau_buckets` upsert needs a stated unique key. | Accepted. `(org_id, period_start)`, carried over from `governed_action_counters_org_period_idx` (§3.9 item 5). |
| R3-I11 | INV-20 must ignore `e2e/.auth/owner.json`. | Accepted (as R3-A6). |
| R3-I12 | INV-09's lint bans every formatter outside `ui/money-format.ts`, but counts need locale formatting too. | Accepted. `money-format.ts` owns count formatting through `formatCount`; INV-09 and §1.4 say so. |
| R3-I13 | Prose: "genuinely" at §1.4; the "Why classes, not a symbol brand" heading; `eslint.tenancy-seams.mjs` needs its repo-root prefix. | Accepted. "except where money is the answer"; the heading is "The brand is a class with an ES private field"; the preamble lists `eslint.tenancy-seams.mjs` as a repo-root file. |
| R3-M1 | (INV-02, as R3-I1: the private base constructor is TS2675; `Object.assign` is not refused.) | Accepted (as R3-I1). |
| R3-M2 | The authorization model relies on IAM denials the runtime never issues below enterprise: `checkIAM` allows every capability when `canAccessACL(tier)` is false (`check-iam.ts:143-182`, `entitlements.ts:93-95`), so `defaultRoles` is not enforced for Free, Build and Scale orgs, and the design deleted `actorCanManageApiKeys` (`cli-actions.ts:55-66`) without a replacement. | Accepted. §1.5 states the bypass; `assertOrgRole` in `packages/iam/src/org-role.ts` (the `resolveActorOrgRole` query moves there so `packages/agent` can call it) throws `HandlerError { code: "forbidden" }`; every rev1 contract with a role restriction calls it in its handler — `authorize_cli` replaces the predicate rather than dropping it, `purchase_gau_bucket`, `set_auto_topup`, `resolve_approval`, `list_api_keys`, the four billing reads — with handler tests against a tier-`free` org (INV-29); the §6.1 `denied` negatives name `forbidden` from the handler; WL-51 keeps the CLAUDE.md:301 guidance in corrected form. |
| R3-M3 | WL-38/39/42 (phase F) add `"app"` to `layers` while `APP_DIR` is still `apps/app_deprecated` (`app-dir.mjs:11`) and `check:ui-parity --strict` runs on every PR (`pipeline.yml:195`, `check_ui_parity.mjs:175-181,201,361`), so each PR fails CI. | Accepted. Every `layers: ["app"]` addition moves into WL-50, the PR that flips `APP_DIR` and lands `apps/app/capability-ui-map.json`; WL-40, WL-41, WL-44, WL-45 acceptance includes `pnpm check:ui-parity --strict` passing with `APP_DIR` unchanged (§8). |
| R3-M4 | `get_gau_bucket` is `mutates: false` yet WL-27 had its handler insert the bucket. | Accepted. The handler computes a virtual bucket from `resolveContractTerms` and never inserts; the recorder's lazy create is the only writer (§3.9 contracts table). |
| R3-M5 | The e2e job builds `apps/app` twice (the `Build` step and the Playwright `next build && next start`). | Accepted. `webServer.command` is `next start` on turbo's output; the INV-22 sentinel scan reads that build (§6.3). |
| R3-M6 | `--pass-with-no-tests` lets a `testMatch` mistake pass green after WL-44. | Accepted. Removed in WL-47; INV-20 names it. |
| R3-M7 | Nothing states what Fleet, Run, People, API keys and Billing render between phase B and phase E, and WL-08's billing `<NotRecorded>` conflicts with INV-18's seven rows. | Accepted. Those routes render `<PageHeader>` with their title and no body until their page PR; `UNRECORDED` keeps its seven rows throughout; WL-08 no longer renders billing as `NotRecorded` (§8, WL-08). |
| R3-M8 | INV-13's JSX rule needs type information no lint rule has. | Accepted. A syntactic `no-restricted-syntax` selector bans a non-literal `href`/`action` on `<a>`/`<Link>`/`<form>` outside `ui/navigation.tsx`; `SafeLink`/`SafeForm` take `SafePath`-typed props (§3.8). |
| R3-M9 | Nothing creates an `org_billing_settings` row for a new org. | Accepted (as R3-A9): an upsert keyed on `org_id`, tested against an org with no row. |
| R3-M10 | `*.type-test.ts` files count as uncovered lines against the 90% floor. | Accepted. `src/**/*.type-test.ts` in `coverage.exclude` (INV-24). |
| R3-M11 | `webServer.env` "lists exactly what the production build needs" while Playwright also passes turbo's env list. | Accepted. Worded as "in addition to the turbo `test:e2e` env list", naming the bootstrap names (§6.3). |
| R3-M12 | Every SSE poll of `get_run` is a full kernel invoke with an IAM query and two ClickHouse writes; no poll budget is stated. | Accepted. `get_run` takes `waitMs` (handler-side long poll, 20 s), `idleMs` 300 000; an idle Run page costs at most three invokes a minute (§3.5). |
| R3-M13 | `remainingGau` "never negative" clamps an overdrawn bucket to 0. | Accepted. The stored value may be negative and the page prints "overdrawn by N" (§1.4, §3.9 item 5). |
| R3-M14 | INV-09 and count formatting. | Accepted (as R3-I12). |
| R3-M15 | Prose at §1.4. | Accepted (as R3-I13). |

The billing amendment itself changed, beyond the above: §0 items 2, 8 and 9; §1.4 (billing-mode and auto top-up sections, invoice kinds); §1.5 (`resolve_approval`'s refusal conditions); §3.2 (`billing_suspended` back on `ActionResult`, with its producer); §3.9 items 5–12 and 14 (buckets carry the invoice and top-up state; two modes on `org_billing_settings`; one settlement ledger; the recorder path; the mode-aware gate; the daily close job; the Stripe objects; `set_auto_topup` and the platform-only `set_org_billing_terms`); INV-27, INV-30, INV-31; §6.1 (recorder, gate, close job and webhook negatives); §8 (WL-30, WL-31, WL-45 added; later items renumbered); and openQuestions (the `hard_stop`-only and auto-top-up-is-future questions removed; the amendment's two assumptions added for the maintainer to override).
### 9.1 Revision-2 objections (revision 3)

"Accepted" means the objection was right on the evidence, re-verified at `27b9d2520`. None was rebutted: every cited line was found as described.

| # | Objection | Resolution |
|---|---|---|
| A′1 | Console reads and SSE polls are metered; Halt's `exhausted` state is unreachable | Accepted. `kernel.ts:1313-1316,1543-1549` confirmed; `tacho.command.dispatch.ts:24` is `noBillingGate`. §1.5 names the metering surface (only `resolve_approval`); INV-28 contract test; `noBillingGate: true` declared on every console read (WL-17–20, `list_orgs`/`list_workspaces` in WL-19) and on the membership writes (WL-22). The `exhausted` UI and its tests move to the approval dialog (WL-40); the `gau_exhausted` branch is deleted from WL-41 (§1.3). |
| A′2 | The GAU purchase never appears in Invoices; no receipt | Accepted. `rg invoice_creation packages/billing/src` empty; `invoices.ts:32-80` the only writer; `webhooks.ts:159-176` receipts on `invoice.paid` only. §3.9 item 10: `invoice_creation` with org metadata; `syncInvoiceFromStripe` resolves the org from `invoice.metadata.org_id` (`stripe-provider.ts:227`); `grants.ts:205-217` ignores it; provider and webhook tests in WL-28; `pay` asserts `invoice_creation.enabled`. |
| A′3 | The contract row freezes the tier and has no source of truth for published figures | Accepted. `schema/billing.ts:18-56` has no rate columns; `subscriptions.ts:37-91` rewrites `plan_id` on every webhook; `plan-allowance.ts:50-99` already joins the entitled plan. §3.9 items 1–3: published terms on `billing.plans`, negotiated terms in `contract_terms`, `resolveContractTerms` picks one live; `create_org` writes nothing billing-shaped (WL-16 ↔ WL-26 coupling removed). |
| A′4 | `included_gau_per_period` has no unit; monthly vs yearly subscribers differ 12× | Accepted. §3.9 item 4: `included_gau_per_month`; revision 3 answered with `months_in_period` (12/1/1), which revision 5 replaced with a one-month bucket for every org (R4-A3) — a yearly and a monthly subscription on the same terms get the same monthly allowance, sliced on the anniversary day; the page prints "per month"; published per-month figures in openQuestions. |
| M′1 | The page-load smoke has three routes with no static title | Accepted. §1.2 oracle column and INV-20: `/` → `expect.redirectTo`; `/invite/[token]` visited as an unknown token asserting `pages.invitationNotFound`, both states exporting the matching `generateMetadata`; `/cli/authorize` carries a valid PKCE query built in `routes.ts` (`cli-authorize.ts:53-67`). |
| M′2 | `pay.spec.ts` never runs: `STRIPE_E2E` is stripped by turbo; no `workflow_dispatch` | Accepted. `turbo.json:181-224` confirmed. §6.3: `STRIPE_E2E` added to `turbo.json`; fail-closed on `main` and same-repo PRs; fork PRs skip with an annotation; the `workflow_dispatch` claim is dropped; a mutation check recorded. |
| M′3 | 53 deprecated specs survive to WL-53 | Accepted. WL-50 deletes `apps/app_deprecated/e2e/**`, its `playwright.config.ts` and the devDependencies only they use; INV-20 walks `apps/*/e2e`. |
| M′4 | Skills and agents still mandate an e2e per flow | Accepted. `oxagen-testing/SKILL.md:53-55,61,70`, `quality-gates/SKILL.md:59-61`, `agents/test-engineer.md:30`, `agents/e2e-runner.md` confirmed. WL-51 rewrites them and reduces `e2e-runner.md` to maintaining the three specs; acceptance is an `rg` over `CLAUDE.md AGENTS.md .claude`. |
| M′5 | `contract_terms` carries three futures (`exhaustion_policy` enum, `stripe_price_id`, retention rate) | Accepted. All three deleted (§3.9 items 2, 6, 13): the behaviour past the allowance is the org's billing mode on `org_billing_settings`, not a column on the terms; `price_data` is the one checkout path; retention is off the page with the settlement path stated in ADR-055. |
| M′6 | WL-27 reshapes `get_action_usage` while `app_deprecated` still reads its fields and CI builds it | Accepted. `handlers/billing.action_usage.ts:31,161` reads the counter WL-24 would drop; `action-usage-panel.tsx:90-91,133-144` reads the fields. The counter drop moves out of WL-24 into WL-27, which introduces `get_gau_bucket` under a new name, retires `get_action_usage` and deletes the deprecated panel and its spec in the same PR, with `tsc` on `@oxagen/app-deprecated` in its acceptance. |
| I′1 | The symbol brand survives spread and `Object.assign` | Accepted, and revision 3's fix was itself wrong on `Object.assign` and on the subclass (R3-I1 above): the brand is now an ES private field with a runtime check in the kernel core (§3.1, INV-02, WL-10). |
| I′2 | The worklist is circular around `shell.context` | Accepted. `live/shell.ts:11-12` is a stub. WL-08 deletes the stub and the port method (switchers render the current org/ws from the viewer); WL-11 implements `shell.context` on `kernelRead` as the seam's first production caller; WL-32 becomes the shell page item (switcher lists, `/` landing, `MobileNav`) depending on WL-11 and WL-13; §7.1 no longer says the stub is kept. |
| I′3 | 23 generated e2e tests; `/` has no title | Accepted. One `test()` with `test.step` per route (§6.3); `expect.redirectTo` admitted only for `src/app/page.tsx`; dynamic routes carry their parameters in `routes.ts` (INV-20). |
| I′4 | INV-05's allowlist names tables the module does not read | Accepted. `tenancy-lookups.ts:8-10,204-209,218-220` and `schema/security.ts:145`, `schema/auth.ts:78,263` confirmed. The allowlist is rewritten to the drizzle exports actually walked, with a column allowlist on `users`, and the test bans `.query.` access and `sql` imports (§3.7, INV-05). |
| I′5 | `login.spec.ts` cannot sign in: email verification is required under `next start` | Accepted. `auth.ts:309`, `local-env.ts:39-64`, `pipeline.yml:334-335` confirmed; `nightly.yml:309` sets `"1"`, which fails the `=== "true"` check. §5: the harness sets `E2E_TEST=true` in `webServer.env`; INV-22 reworded to what is enforced; the seed performs a sign-in and fails loudly otherwise (WL-46). |
| N′ (A) | `createGauCheckout` duplicates `createPaymentCheckout`'s `{price}` branch; `stripe_price_id` has no caller | Accepted: `price_data` only, one method, one test (§3.9). |
| N′ (A) | `exhaustion_policy` values without a recorder path | Accepted: no column. Revision 3 answered with `hard_stop` only; the maintainer's amendment replaced that with the billing mode on the org's billing row and a recorder path for each mode (§3.9 items 6–8). |
| N′ (A) | INV-25's `*Micros*` wildcard fails on legitimate mapper reads | Accepted: fixed denylist of the four names (INV-25). |
| N′ (A) | Webhook grant depends on the handler's pending row | Accepted: rate and currency in the session metadata; `grantGauPurchaseForCheckout` is an upsert keyed on the session id (§3.9). |
| N′ (A) | Retention priced with no charger | Accepted: no rate column; and per R3-A3 the retention slice is off the page entirely, and the dead charger is deleted (§3.9 item 13). |
| N′ (A) | `ensureStripeCustomer` mints a customer per purchase for a Free org | Accepted in effect; the stated reason was wrong (R3-A9: the existing metadata search is eventually consistent, so the duplicate is a race, not a certainty). `org_billing_settings.stripe_customer_id` through an upsert (WL-24, WL-28). |
| N′ (A) | Purchase total arithmetic seam; handler recomputes | Accepted: `mulMicros` in `money.ts`; handler recomputes at submit (§3.9). |
| N′ (A) | `pay` can assert `invoice_creation.enabled` | Accepted (§6.3). |
| N′ (M) | `pay` asserts the rate block before submitting | Accepted: removed from `pay`; the contract-rate component test carries it (§6.1). |
| N′ (M) | Login harness needs `E2E_TEST=true`; INV-22 overstated | Accepted (§5, INV-22). |
| N′ (M) | `insufficient_credits`/`billing_suspended` have no producer | Accepted for `insufficient_credits`. `billing_suspended` keeps a producer: `assertGauAvailable` keeps the dunning-suspension refusal (§3.9 item 9), so it stays on `ActionResult` and in the approval dialog. |
| N′ (M) | `authorize_cli` has no API caller | Accepted: `surfaces: []`, layers without `api`, no API route (§3.7, WL-21). |
| N′ (M) | `meterMode` has no reader | Accepted: dropped from `get_gau_bucket`. |
| N′ (M) | `MC_DATA` residue in `turbo.json`, `env-check.ts`, `.gitignore` | Accepted: WL-04 paths (§5). |
| N′ (M) | `pipeline.yml:319` misdescribed | Accepted: §7.1 describes the actual edit. |
| N′ (M) | Arch tests shell out to `fd` | Accepted: `fs.readdir`/`tinyglobby`; the post-build scan is a Node step (§2, INV-22). |
| N′ (M) | No subscription-upgrade path in rev1 | Recorded in openQuestions for confirmation; the design does not add one. |
| N′ (M) | Fourteen NotRecorded slots ship as product surface | Accepted in part: in-page rows render nothing except `run.frames_wrapped`; the six page routes stay (§3.6, INV-18). |
| N′ (I) | `kernelRead` can refuse mutating contracts at compile time | Accepted: `ReadContract` (§3.2). |
| N′ (I) | `RESERVED_ORG_SLUGS` lives in a file WL-07 deletes | Accepted: moved into the `create_org` contract (WL-16). |
| N′ (I) | `catalog-used` must expand template keys; `AppConfig.Messages` needs a hand-listed intersection | Accepted: INV-12 names the expansion and a generated, CI-checked `messages.d.ts` (WL-13). |
| N′ (I) | Probes and test-only modules need eslint/tsconfig/knip exclusions | Accepted: §4 preamble, WL-13. |
| N′ (I) | INV-13 misses `location.*`, computed `href`/`action` | Accepted (§3.8). |
| N′ (I) | INV-08 enforces one direction | Accepted: reverse `@ts-expect-error` (§3.4). |
| N′ (I) | `surface_denied` is unreachable; `opts.surface` never passed | Accepted: stated in §3.2 step 3; the row is dropped. |
| N′ (I) | `fd`/`rg` in arch tests and pipeline steps | Accepted (as above). |
| N′ (I) | List the production-build webServer env | Accepted (§6.3). |

### 9.2 Revision-1 objections (carried from revision 2)

| # | Objection | Resolution |
|---|---|---|
| A1–A11 | Billing out of rev1; no bucket backing; no unit-quantity purchase; no contracted rate; dollar usage would leak; invoices have no contract; buy-more blocks itself; exhaustion becomes an error; checkout redirect has no seam; pay e2e cannot run in CI; no ADR or spec amendment | Accepted; carried into §1.4, §3.8, §3.9, INV-25, INV-27, phase D2, WL-38, WL-44, WL-48. |
| M1–M9 | 14 journeys; INV-20 forbade a parameterized smoke; no pay path; seed served deleted journeys; e2e coverage collection; WL-03 incomplete; no a11y gate; manifest demands an e2e per capability; no title oracle | Accepted; carried into §5, §6, INV-20, INV-26, WL-03, WL-49–48, WL-50, WL-51. |
| I1–I15 | `inviteeCtx` minted an `OrgCtx`; `SystemLookups` kept table reads contracts cover; CLI approve path; INV-03 subpaths; INV-05 denylist; `z.output<>` → `unknown`; INV-14 vs plain `Error`; fabricated zeros; INV-19 file selection; INV-13 CLI flow; INV-01 api routes; static cap; deletion order; guards vanish for 26 PRs; kernel imports what its layer forbids | Accepted; carried into §2, §3.1, §3.2, §3.4, §3.7, §3.8, INV-01, INV-03, INV-05, INV-08, INV-13, INV-14, INV-19, WL-05, WL-21, WL-22, WL-46. |
| N-GAU | Who may buy GAUs | Every tier, including Free: a block is a unit purchase at the org's contracted rate, not a credit pack; the credits Free-tier gate (`entitlements.ts:125-127`) stays on the credits path the app no longer uses. In openQuestions for ratification. |
